  894| // Bounded raw-to-output checks are independent semantic assertions, not a reducer.
  895| fn accepts_usage_axis(row: &Value, launch: &Value, rates: &Value, a: &Value) -> bool {
  896|     match price_range(row, launch, rates) {
  897|         None => a["valuation"]["state"] != "valued" && a["replacement"]["eligible"] != true,
  898|         Some((lo, hi, complete)) => {
  899|             a["valuation"]["state"] == "valued"
  900|                 && a["valuation"]["low"] == lo.to_string()
  901|                 && a["valuation"]["high"] == hi.to_string()
  902|                 && a["valuation"]["complete"] == complete
  903|                 && (a["replacement"]["eligible"] != true || complete)
  904|         }
  905|     }
  906| }
  907| fn accepts_retention_axis(a: &Value, floor: i128) -> bool {
  908|     a["exposure"]["high"]["kind"] == "at_least"
  909|         && a["exposure"]["high"]["nano"]
  910|             .as_str()
  911|             .and_then(|s| s.parse::<i128>().ok())
  912|             .is_some_and(|n| n >= floor)
  913| }
  914| #[test]
  915| fn r7_semantically_wrong_consistent_outputs_are_rejected() {
  916|     let rates = json!({"openai":read(root().join("oracle/rates/openai_api_rates.json")),"anthropic":read(root().join("oracle/rates/anthropic_api_rates.json"))});
  917|     let c = corpus_case("P130-R01-usage-incomplete-terminal");
  918|     let sess = &c["inputs"]["sessions"][0];
  919|     let row: Value =
  920|         serde_json::from_str(sess["logs"]["turn-1"]["lines"][1].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
  921|     let a = &c["expected"]["attempts"][0];
  922|     assert!(accepts_usage_axis(&row, &sess["launch"], &rates, a));
  923|     let mut wrong = a.clone();
  924|     wrong["valuation"]["complete"] = json!(true);
  925|     wrong["replacement"] = json!({"eligible":true,"reason":"UsageBound"});
  926|     wrong["exposure"]["high"] =
  927|         json!({"kind":"exact","nano":wrong["valuation"]["high"],"basis":"usage_valuation"});
  928|     wrong["billing"] = json!("established");
  929|     wrong["blocking"] = json!([]);
  930|     assert!(!accepts_usage_axis(&row, &sess["launch"], &rates, &wrong));
  931|     let raw = json!({"launch":{"auth_lane":"subscription"},"reservation":{"event":"usd_reserved","rid":1,"reserved_usd":0.5}});
  932|     let floor = dollars(&raw["reservation"]["reserved_usd"]).unwrap();
  933|     let a = json!({"exposure":{"high":{"kind":"at_least","nano":"500000000"}},"billing":"unresolved","blocking":["LaneContradiction","UnresolvedExposure"]});
  934|     assert!(accepts_retention_axis(&a, floor));
  935|     let mut wrong = a.clone();
  936|     wrong["exposure"]["high"] = json!({"kind":"exact","nano":"0","basis":"none"});
  937|     wrong["billing"] = json!("established");
  938|     wrong["blocking"] = json!([]);
  939|     assert!(!accepts_retention_axis(&wrong, floor));
  940|     let c = corpus_case("VC02-anthropic-usage-settlement");
  941|     let sess = &c["inputs"]["sessions"][0];
  942|     let mut row: Value =
  943|         serde_json::from_str(sess["logs"]["turn-1"]["lines"][1].as_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
  944|     let a = &c["expected"]["attempts"][0];
  945|     assert!(accepts_usage_axis(&row, &sess["launch"], &rates, a));
  946|     row["observed_tier"] = json!("default");
  947|     assert!(
  948|         !accepts_usage_axis(&row, &sess["launch"], &rates, a),
  949|         "unchanged internally consistent numeric output cannot repair invalid raw tier"
  950|     );
  951|     let c = corpus_case("P130-R01-usage-incomplete-terminal");
  952|     let e = &c["evaluations"][0]["expected"];
  953|     let mut wrong = e["objectives"][0].clone();
  954|     wrong["provisional"] = json!(false);
  955|     assert_ne!(
  956|         wrong["provisional"],
  957|         json!(provisional(
  958|             c["expected"]["attempts"].as_array().unwrap(),
  959|             e["attribution"].as_array().unwrap(),
  960|             &wrong
  961|         ))
  962|     );
  963| }

===== BEGIN FILE oracle/README.md | sha256 866b2a3b9e409c962a6707c4312c59e26c76de7fed8d2a86617160c3d5a74769 | 5147 bytes | 19 lines =====
    1| # Independent raw-axis qualification
    2| 
    3| This lane is separately authored from canonical definitions and generator logic. It binds raw filtered revision5 inputs, the independently frozen audit facts (`f097e5a541b279f2081c71f54ca3403151475348b8a30245e6846708590e293e`), pinned rates, and root policy approval07303d5c. Frozen originals are copied byte-for-byte in frozen-raw/. The test source imports only serde_json, sha2 and the Rust standard library; it imports no canonical semantic helper and calculates no full reducer result.
    4| 
    5| Run the `independent_raw_axes` Cargo integration test. `ORACLE_REPORT=<path>` optionally writes qualification.json. Every original173case has explicit raw-selection and full-C-K standing. The latter remains unestablished. Source/line assertions cover all137raw-selected cases (105current-producer,32future-profile),142sources,287rows,147lexical reservation groups;104observations have independently recomputed exact usage extrema. Future profile projection follows the frozen selection, never feeds it. The other2future-profile cases have no selected dollar source. Missing-launch dollar evidence is additionally covered as an unresolved control; it is not assigned an invented launch identity. New controls are independently checked outside the historical173case count.
    6| 
    7| Supported axes: exact raw/rate/fact hashes; source selection and source/line totality; maximum distinct well-formed reservation amounts; unique comparison applicability; provider-qualified identities and explicit declared family; billing/auxiliary field states; required counts; feasible OpenAI cache vertices; Anthropic lifetime/residual bounds; complete status200 AND successful provider terminal AND counts conjunction; error-status usage retention; malformed IDs; historical revision findings and writer arithmetic controls. Single-observation expected valuations are compared to separately recomputed raw arithmetic. Multi-observation reducer aggregation, complete correlation/transition semantics, full K findings and production gate authority remain unestablished.
    8| 
    9| qualification.json uses established/contradicted/unestablished standing. The assertions fail on contradictions; they do not rewrite expectations. A passing raw-axis check is not full C-K acceptance, a reference reducer, production integration, freeze approval or S2a readiness.
   10| 
   11| Future gate controls consume only a separate top-level trusted_fixture_inputs mapping with an exact payload digest, authority, session, policy reference and evidence reference. Outputs say test_mock. Producer writer labels, verified:true, provenance labels and self-supplied receipt digests cannot mint this input. This tests conditional policy and payload binding only. No production verifier, cryptography, authentication channel or enforcement is implemented.
   12|

   44|   680| def usage_usd(rates: dict, *, input_tokens, cached, cache_write, output_tokens, multiplier) -> float:
   45|   681|     """Same formula as usage_ledger.codex_usd, times the tier multiplier."""
   46|   682|     long = input_tokens > rates["long_input_threshold"]
   47|   683|     m_in, m_out = (rates["long_input_multiplier"], rates["long_output_multiplier"]) if long else (1, 1)
   48|   684|     base = ((input_tokens - cached - cache_write) * rates["input"] + cached * rates["cached_input"]
   49|   685|             + cache_write * rates["cache_write"]) * m_in + output_tokens * rates["output"] * m_out
   50|   686|     return base / 1e6 * multiplier
   51|   687| 
   52|   688| 
   53|   689| def reserve_usd(rates: dict, *, input_bound: int, max_output_tokens: int, multiplier: float) -> float:
   54|   690|     """Worst case: every input token at the dearest input rate (a cache can always miss)."""
   55|   691|     long = input_bound > rates["long_input_threshold"]
   56|   692|     m_in, m_out = (rates["long_input_multiplier"], rates["long_output_multiplier"]) if long else (1, 1)
   57|   693|     dearest = max(rates["input"], rates["cache_write"])
   58|   694|     return (input_bound * dearest * m_in + max_output_tokens * rates["output"] * m_out) / 1e6 * multiplier

   61|   697| def _canonical(value) -> bytes:
   62|   698|     return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()

   65|   701| def input_basis(body: dict, items_key: str = "input") -> dict:
   66|   702|     """Byte sizes and a hash chain over the request's input items
   67|   703|     (`input` for Responses, `messages` for Anthropic Messages).
   68|   704| 
   69|   705|     chains[k] identifies the first k items, so a later request can be checked
   70|   706|     for starting with an earlier request's whole input. A token is at least
   71|   707|     one byte of its text and JSON escaping only adds bytes, so a canonical
   72|   708|     item's byte length bounds its text tokens.
   73|   709|     """
   74|   710|     items = body.get(items_key, [])
   75|   711|     if not isinstance(items, list):
   76|   712|         items = [items]
   77|   713|     rest = _canonical({k: v for k, v in body.items() if k != items_key})
   78|   714|     h = hashlib.sha256(b"").digest()
   79|   715|     chains, sizes = [h.hex()], []
   80|   716|     for item in items:
   81|   717|         raw = _canonical(item)
   82|   718|         sizes.append(len(raw))
   83|   719|         h = hashlib.sha256(h + hashlib.sha256(raw).digest()).digest()
   84|   720|         chains.append(h.hex())
   85|   721|     return {"items": len(items), "chains": chains, "item_bytes": sizes,
   86|   722|             "rest_sha256": hashlib.sha256(rest).hexdigest(), "rest_bytes": len(rest)}
   87|   723| 
   88|   724| 
   89|   725| def input_bound(basis: dict, prefix: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str]:
   90|   726|     """Upper bound on the request's input tokens, and how it was found.
   91|   727| 
   92|   728|     prefix: the provider's input_tokens for an earlier request whose input
   93|   729|     this request starts with. Those tokens already cover its instructions and
   94|   730|     tools; changed ones are counted again in full.

   97|   733|     if prefix and isinstance(prefix.get("input_tokens"), int) and prefix["items"] <= n \
   98|   734|             and basis["chains"][prefix["items"]] == prefix["chain_sha256"]:
   99|   735|         k = prefix["items"]

  102|   738|             bound += basis["rest_bytes"] + ITEM_OVERHEAD_TOKENS
  103|   739|         return bound, "prefix"
  104|   740|     return basis["rest_bytes"] + sum(sizes) + ITEM_OVERHEAD_TOKENS * (n + 1), "bytes"

  127|   868| class SubscriptionProxyBudget:
  128|   869|     """Serialized stopping threshold, with no reservation or hard output cap.
  129|   870| 
  130|   871|     The proxy owns locking and durably writes admission before any upstream effect.
  131|   872|     An unknown exchange keeps its admission unresolved and permanently stops this session.
  132|   873|     """
  133|   874|     max_output_tokens = None
  134|   875| 
  135|   876|     def __init__(self, *, max_usd, rates, spent_before_usd=0.0):
  136|   877|         self.max_usd, self.rates = max_usd, rates
  137|   878|         self.settled = spent_before_usd
  138|   879|         self.inflight = None
  139|   880|         self.exhausted = spent_before_usd >= max_usd
  140|   881|         self.unknown = False
  141|   882|         self.serial = 0
  142|   883| 
  143|   884|     def admit(self):
  144|   885|         if self.exhausted:
  145|   886|             return None, "usd_limit: subscription proxy threshold reached or accounting unknown"
  146|   887|         if self.inflight is not None:
  147|   888|             return None, "subscription_proxy_request_in_flight"
  148|   889|         self.serial += 1
  149|   890|         self.inflight = self.serial
  150|   891|         return {"rid": self.serial}, None
  151|   892| 
  152|   893|     def settle(self, ticket, *, http_status, response, terminal, stream_error):
  153|   894|         usage = response.get("usage")
  154|   895|         reason = None
  155|   896|         if (http_status != 200 or stream_error or terminal not in ("response.completed", "response.done")):
  156|   897|             reason = "exchange_not_completed"
  157|   898|         elif (response.get("model") != self.rates["model"] or not isinstance(response.get("id"), str)
  158|   899|               or not response["id"].strip()):
  159|   900|             reason = "response_identity_not_established"
  160|   901|         elif response.get("status") not in (None, "completed") or response.get("error"):
  161|   902|             reason = "exchange_not_completed"
  162|   903|         elif not isinstance(usage, dict):
  163|   904|             reason = "usage_not_established"
  164|   905|         else:
  165|   906|             counts = [usage.get(k) for k in ("input_tokens", "output_tokens")]
  166|   907|             if not all(type(v) is int and v >= 0 for v in counts):
  167|   908|                 reason = "usage_not_established"
  168|   909|         amount, basis = None, None
  169|   910|         if reason is None:
  170|   911|             i, o = counts
  171|   912|             details = usage.get("input_tokens_details")
  172|   913|             if details is not None and not isinstance(details, dict):
  173|   914|                 reason = "invalid_cache_usage"
  174|   915|             else:
  175|   916|                 details = details or {}
  176|   917|                 c, w = details.get("cached_tokens"), details.get("cache_write_tokens")
  177|   918|                 if any(v is not None and (type(v) is not int or v < 0 or v > i) for v in (c, w)) or (c or 0) + (w or 0) > i:
  178|   919|                     reason = "invalid_cache_usage"
  179|   920|                 else:
  180|   921|                     if c is None or w is None:
  181|   922|                         # Missing optional cache counts: value all input at the dearer rate.
  182|   923|                         c, w = 0, i if self.rates["cache_write"] > self.rates["input"] else 0
  183|   924|                         basis = "conservative_all_input_at_dearest_rate"

  207|   948| class UsdBudget:
  208|   949|     """A session dollar limit, enforced per request. Callers hold the proxy lock.
  209|   950| 
  210|   951|     reserve() sets aside the request's worst-case cost or refuses; settle()
  211|   952|     replaces it with the charge from the provider's final usage. Reservations
  212|   953|     use the requested tier's multiplier (the dearer of it and default with
  213|   954|     strict_tier). A response served at another tier is charged at that tier,
  214|   955|     and later reservations use at least the dearer of it and default; a
  215|   956|     response reporting no tier is charged at the dearest multiplier, which
  216|   957|     then becomes that floor. After the first refusal every later request is
  217|   958|     refused.
  218|   959|     """
  219|   960| 
  220|   961|     def __init__(self, *, max_usd: float, rates: dict, tier: str | None, max_output_tokens: int,
  221|   962|                  spent_before_usd: float = 0.0, prefix: dict | None = None,
  222|   963|                  multiplier_floor: float = 0.0, strict_tier: bool = False):
  223|   964|         if (tier or "default") not in rates["tier_multiplier"]:
  224|   965|             raise ContainerError("usage", f"a dollar limit needs a priced tier, not {tier!r}")
  225|   966|         self.max_usd, self.rates, self.tier = max_usd, rates, tier or "default"
  226|   967|         self.max_output_tokens, self.strict_tier = max_output_tokens, strict_tier
  227|   968|         self.settled, self.outstanding = spent_before_usd, 0.0
  228|   969|         self.prefix, self.floor = prefix, multiplier_floor
  229|   970|         self.exhausted, self.seq = False, 0
  230|   971| 
  231|   972|     def multiplier(self) -> float:
  232|   973|         table = self.rates["tier_multiplier"]
  233|   974|         m = max(table[self.tier], table["default"]) if self.strict_tier else table[self.tier]
  234|   975|         return max(m, self.floor)
  235|   976| 
  236|   977|     def worst_case(self, bound: int) -> float:
  237|   978|         return reserve_usd(self.rates, input_bound=bound, max_output_tokens=self.max_output_tokens,
  238|   979|                            multiplier=self.multiplier())
  239|   980| 
  240|   981|     def reserve(self, basis: dict):
  241|   982|         """(ticket, None) when the request may go, or (None, refusal detail)."""
  242|   983|         bound, how = input_bound(basis, self.prefix)
  243|   984|         amount = self.worst_case(bound)
  244|   985|         if self.exhausted or self.settled + self.outstanding + amount > self.max_usd:
  245|   986|             self.exhausted = True
  246|   987|             return None, (f"usd_limit: spent {self.settled + self.outstanding:.4f} + worst case {amount:.4f} "
  247|   988|                           f"> limit {self.max_usd:.4f}")
  248|   989|         self.seq += 1
  249|   990|         self.outstanding += amount
  250|   991|         return {"rid": self.seq, "reserved_usd": amount, "input_bound": bound, "bound_basis": how,
  251|   992|                 "basis": basis}, None
  252|   993| 
  253|   994|     def settle(self, ticket: dict, *, http_status, response: dict) -> dict:
  254|   995|         """Record the charge. http_status None: the upstream exchange failed part way."""
  255|   996|         self.outstanding -= ticket["reserved_usd"]
  256|   997|         table = self.rates["tier_multiplier"]
  257|   998|         usage = response.get("usage") or {}
  258|   999|         details = usage.get("input_tokens_details") or {}
  259|  1000|         i, o = usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens")
  260|  1001|         c, w = details.get("cached_tokens") or 0, details.get("cache_write_tokens") or 0
  261|  1002|         if http_status is not None and http_status != 200:
  262|  1003|             # An error status before any stream: treated as unbilled (assumed, not verified).
  263|  1004|             charged, how = 0.0, "error_status"
  264|  1005|         elif all(isinstance(x, int) and x >= 0 for x in (i, o, c, w)) and c + w <= i:
  265|  1006|             observed = response.get("service_tier")
  266|  1007|             m = table[observed] if observed in table else max(table.values())
  267|  1008|             if observed != self.tier:
  268|  1009|                 self.floor = max(self.floor, table["default"], m)
  269|  1010|             charged, how = usage_usd(self.rates, input_tokens=i, cached=c, cache_write=w, output_tokens=o,
  270|  1011|                                      multiplier=m), "usage"
  271|  1012|             basis = ticket["basis"]
  272|  1013|             self.prefix = {"items": basis["items"], "chain_sha256": basis["chains"][-1],
  273|  1014|                            "rest_sha256": basis["rest_sha256"], "input_tokens": i}
  274|  1015|         else:
  275|  1016|             charged, how = ticket["reserved_usd"], "reservation"
  276|  1017|         self.settled += charged
  277|  1018|         return {"rid": ticket["rid"], "reserved_usd": round(ticket["reserved_usd"], 6),
  278|  1019|                 "charged_usd": round(charged, 6), "charge_basis": how,
  279|  1020|                 "session_spent_usd": round(self.settled + self.outstanding, 6), "multiplier_floor": self.floor}

  282|  1023| class AnthropicUsdBudget(UsdBudget):
  283|  1024|     """UsdBudget for Anthropic Messages, with the usage ledger's rates (native_usd).
  284|  1025| 
  285|  1026|     No tiers: the proxy refuses service_tier and speed, so a request runs at
  286|  1027|     standard rates; a response reporting another tier keeps its reservation.
  287|  1028|     Worst case: every input token at the dearest of the input and both
  288|  1029|     cache-write rates, plus the output cap (thinking counts as output). Cache
  289|  1030|     writes are charged by lifetime; writes the provider did not split by
  290|  1031|     lifetime are charged at the dearer rate. No long-context rule is applied,
  291|  1032|     as in the ledger.
  292|  1033|     """
  293|  1034| 
  294|  1035|     def multiplier(self) -> float:
  295|  1036|         return 1.0
  296|  1037| 
  297|  1038|     def worst_case(self, bound: int) -> float:
  298|  1039|         r = self.rates
  299|  1040|         dearest = max(r["input"], r["cache_write_5m"], r["cache_write_1h"])
  300|  1041|         return (bound * dearest + self.max_output_tokens * r["output"]) / 1e6
  301|  1042| 
  302|  1043|     def settle(self, ticket: dict, *, http_status, response: dict) -> dict:
  303|  1044|         self.outstanding -= ticket["reserved_usd"]
  304|  1045|         u, r = response.get("usage") or {}, self.rates
  305|  1046|         i, cr, cw, o = (u.get(k) for k in ("input_tokens", "cache_read_input_tokens",
  306|  1047|                                              "cache_creation_input_tokens", "output_tokens"))
  307|  1048|         w5, w1 = u.get("ephemeral_5m_input_tokens") or 0, u.get("ephemeral_1h_input_tokens") or 0
  308|  1049|         total = None
  309|  1050|         if http_status is not None and http_status != 200:
  310|  1051|             charged, how = 0.0, "error_status"  # assumed unbilled, not verified
  311|  1052|         elif http_status is not None \
  312|  1053|                 and all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and x >= 0 for x in (i, cr, cw, o, w5, w1)) \
  313|  1054|                 and w5 + w1 <= cw and u.get("service_tier") in (None, "standard"):
  314|  1055|             # http_status None: the stream ended before message_stop, so the
  315|  1056|             # usage seen so far (message_start) understates output; keep the reservation.
  316|  1057|             charged = (i * r["input"] + cr * r["cache_read"] + w5 * r["cache_write_5m"] + w1 * r["cache_write_1h"]
  317|  1058|                        + (cw - w5 - w1) * max(r["cache_write_5m"], r["cache_write_1h"]) + o * r["output"]) / 1e6
  318|  1059|             how, total = "usage", i + cr + cw
  319|  1060|             basis = ticket["basis"]
  320|  1061|             self.prefix = {"items": basis["items"], "chain_sha256": basis["chains"][-1],
  321|  1062|                            "rest_sha256": basis["rest_sha256"], "input_tokens": total}
  322|  1063|         else:
  323|  1064|             charged, how = ticket["reserved_usd"], "reservation"
  324|  1065|         self.settled += charged
  325|  1066|         out = {"rid": ticket["rid"], "reserved_usd": round(ticket["reserved_usd"], 6),
  326|  1067|                "charged_usd": round(charged, 6), "charge_basis": how,
  327|  1068|                "session_spent_usd": round(self.settled + self.outstanding, 6), "multiplier_floor": self.floor}
  328|  1069|         if total is not None:
  329|  1070|             out["input_total"] = total
  330|  1071|         return out

===== BEGIN FILE validator/validate_corpus.py | sha256 12cb4f55d21b0797b2932606fb9d0abb6a4a8e95e5a4c9880f301d4ff7f514e9 | 90353 bytes | 1242 lines =====
    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| """Standalone validator for the Pi attempt-accounting corpus (`pi-accounting-corpus/1`, contract 1.6-s1, revision 7).
    3| 
    4| It is a development tool, not a reducer, and shares no code with any future implementation. It never derives an
    5| expected outcome from inputs except where a check is stated as arithmetic (price derivations from the rate files),
    6| as a contract rule over raw producer rows (forwarding, execution, tier sets, lifecycle facts, obligation intervals,
    7| closure state), or as internal consistency (expected fields that the contract ties together). Standard library only,
    8| deterministic, offline.
    9| 
   10|   validate_corpus.py check      <corpus_dir> <rates_dir>                  validate the corpus itself
   11|   validate_corpus.py results    <corpus_dir> <results.json> [--scope s2a] grade an implementation's result set
   12|   validate_corpus.py selftest   <corpus_dir> <rates_dir>                  prove defective result sets fail, each for its reason
   13|   validate_corpus.py revisions  <corpus_dir> <previous_corpus_dir>        every changed field is a logged revision-6 entry
   14|   validate_corpus.py s2a-subset <corpus_dir>                              print the S2a grading subset (selection rule)
   15| 
   16| Exit status 0 = pass, 1 = failures (listed), 2 = usage."""
   17| import copy, glob, hashlib, json, os, re, sys
   18| from decimal import Decimal
   19| from fractions import Fraction
   20| 
   21| SCHEMA, CONTRACT, PREVIOUS_CONTRACT = 'pi-accounting-corpus/1', 'pi-accounting-contract/1.6-s1', 'pi-accounting-contract/1.5-s1'
   22| CLASSES = {'producer_valid', 'historical_legacy', 'malformed', 'adversarial_synthetic', 'attribution_uncertain', 'future_vertical'}
   23| PROFILES = {'current_producer', 'future_qualified_gate'}
   24| REACH = {'producer_reachable', 'producer_unreachable', 'legacy', 'not_established'}
   25| ROLES = {'finding', 'control', 'valid_control'}
   26| DECISIONS = {'absent', 'valid', 'rejected', 'conflicting', 'not_applicable'}
   27| BASES = {'usage', 'reservation', 'upstream_error', 'error_status'}
   28| REJECT = {'ReservationConflict', 'NoReservation', 'Order', 'AttemptNumber', 'AmountType', 'ReservedMismatch', 'BasisMissing',
   29|           'BasisUnknown', 'KeepAll', 'ErrorCharge', 'StatusType', 'UsageUnpriceable', 'ModelMismatch', 'Undercharge', 'ToleranceRange'}
   30| OBLIGATION = {('closed', 'settlement_amount'), ('closed', 'reservation_confirmed'), ('closed', 'reservation_bound'),
   31|               ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'), ('none', 'none')}
   32| BLOCKING = {'SettlementRejected', 'SettlementConflict', 'ReservationConflict', 'SettlementWithoutReservation',
   33|             'IdentityContradiction', 'LaneContradiction', 'UnadmittedMeasurement', 'UnaccountedAttempt', 'UnresolvedExposure',
   34|             'UnreadableEvidence', 'InventoryIncomplete', 'DuplicateResponseIdentity', 'ArithmeticFailure', 'RateTableUnusable',
   35|             'AttributionContradiction', 'ReopenedWithoutAdmission', 'CommitmentInvalid', 'MalformedEvidence', 'UnadmittedExecution',
   36|             'UnenforcedCommitment', 'GateContradicted'}
   37| OBJ_REASONS = BLOCKING | {'OwnershipUncertain', 'HistoricalUnadmitted', 'PlacementUnplaceable', 'PlacementStraddles', 'ClosureNotEstablished'}
   38| DIAGNOSTICS = {'RepeatedIdenticalRow', 'InconsistentCacheSplit', 'NonBillingUnverified', 'IdentityAbsent', 'ProducerLogCopy',
   39|                'ErrorStatusSettlement', 'ReadFailurePaired', 'PartialUsage', 'UnfinishedAdmission', 'UncertainCodexCall'}
   40| LOW_BASES = {'none', 'usage_valuation'}
   41| HIGH_BASES = {'none', 'usage_valuation', 'proxy_charge', 'proxy_reservation'}
   42| PLACEMENTS = {'inside', 'before', 'after', 'straddles_start', 'straddles_closure', 'unplaceable', 'uncertain', 'not_evaluated'}
   43| OWNERSHIP = {'owned', 'uncertain', 'unadmitted', 'unowned'}
   44| FORWARDING = {'forwarded', 'unknown'}                 # NotForwarded requests are non-attempt evidence (contract 6.1)
   45| KEY_JOIN = {'exact', 'ambiguous', 'unattributed'}     # contract 4: the key join; RS rules are auxiliary rows
   46| SHAPE_RULES = {'RS-1', 'RS-2', 'RS-3'}
   47| EXECUTION = {'terminal', 'not_terminal'}
   48| BILLING = {'established', 'unresolved', 'not_applicable'}
   49| TERMINAL_EVENTS = {'request', 'upstream_error', 'stream_error', 'response_model_refused'}
   50| COMMIT_STATES = {'pending', 'launched_open', 'retired', 'retired_resumable', 'released', 'reopened', 'invalid'}
   51| OPEN_COMMIT = {'pending', 'launched_open', 'retired_resumable', 'reopened'}
   52| LIFE = {'open', 'retired', 'contradicted'}
   53| INVARIANTS = ['INV-F1', 'INV-F2', 'INV-F3', 'INV-F4', 'INV-F5', 'INV-F6', 'INV-F7', 'INV-F8', 'INV-F9', 'INV-F10', 'INV-F11', 'INV-F12', 'INV-F13',
   54|               'INV-T1', 'INV-T2', 'INV-T3', 'INV-T4', 'INV-T5', 'INV-A1', 'INV-A2', 'INV-A3', 'INV-A4', 'INV-A5', 'INV-A6', 'INV-A7', 'INV-A8', 'INV-A9', 'INV-A10']
   55| KEY = re.compile(r'^(res|row|adm):(S\d)/turn-(\d+):(rid|line)=(\d+)$')
   56| REF = re.compile(r'^(S\d)/turn-(\d+):(\d+)$')
   57| STAMP = re.compile(r'^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\dZ$')
   58| MIN_BLOCKED = 25
   59| # revision 4 (OQ-1): admitted valid controls are counted per profile. Total minimum 15 is kept; the current producer
   60| # can only admit sessionless or Codex cases, so metered positives live in the future profile (no padding of either).
   61| MIN_ADMITTED = {'current_producer': 1, 'future_qualified_gate': 11}; MIN_VALID_ADMITTED = 15
   62| ADDED_IN = {'1.1-s1', '1.2-s1', '1.3-s1', '1.4-s1', '1.5-s1', '1.6-s1'}
   63| REPLACEMENT_REASONS = {'UsageBound', 'NotUsageSettlement', 'UsageIncomplete', 'UsageUnpriceable', 'NoReservation'}
   64| TIE = {'usage_valuation': 0, 'proxy_reservation': 1, 'proxy_charge': 2}     # X-0 (1.4-s1)
   65| COVERS = ['failures', 'forwarding', 'partial_streams', 'retries', 'settlement']
   66| PROVIDERS = {'openai': 'openai', 'openai-codex': 'openai', 'anthropic': 'anthropic'}
   67| RANK = {'admitted': 0, 'refused': 1, 'not_established': 1}     # OQ-2: refused and not_established are equally non-permissive
   68| VERTICAL = 'vertical_admission_authority'
   69| MECHS = ('new_forwarding', 'retries', 'reopening', 'billing_bound')
   70| 
   71| 
   72| class Failures(list):
   73|     def add(self, case, msg): self.append(f'{case}: {msg}')
   74| 
   75| 
   76| def nano(s):
   77|     if not isinstance(s, str) or not re.fullmatch(r'-?\d+', s): raise ValueError(f'not a nano integer string: {s!r}')
   78|     return int(s)
   79| 
   80| 
   81| def load_rates(rates_dir):
   82|     out = {}
   83|     for name in ('openai_api_rates.json', 'anthropic_api_rates.json'):
   84|         raw = open(os.path.join(rates_dir, name), 'rb').read()
   85|         out[name] = (hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), json.loads(raw, parse_float=Decimal, parse_int=Decimal))
   86|     return out
   87| 
   88| 
   89| def per_token(rate, mult=1):
   90|     v = Fraction(rate) * Fraction(mult) * Fraction(10 ** 9, 10 ** 6)
   91|     if v.denominator != 1: raise ValueError('rate not exact in nano per token')
   92|     return int(v)
   93| 
   94| 
   95| def price(d, rates):
   96|     """Arithmetic of one derivation record against the rate files (the contract's pricing primitives, 7.1)."""
   97|     u, m, mult = d['usage'], d['model'], Fraction(Decimal(d['tier_multiplier']))
   98|     if d['provider'] == 'openai':
   99|         oa = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]; r = oa['models'][m]
  100|         i, o = u['input_tokens'], u['output_tokens']; c, w = u.get('cached_tokens') or 0, u.get('cache_write_tokens') or 0
  101|         long_ = i > int(oa['long_input_threshold'])
  102|         mi = Fraction(oa['long_input_multiplier']) if long_ else 1; mo = Fraction(oa['long_output_multiplier']) if long_ else 1
  103|         return ((i - c - w) * per_token(r['input'], mult * mi) + c * per_token(r['cached_input'], mult * mi)
  104|                 + w * per_token(r['cache_write'], mult * mi) + o * per_token(r['output'], mult * mo))
  105|     an = rates['anthropic_api_rates.json'][1]; r = an['models'][m]
  106|     i, cr, cw, o = (u.get(k, 0) for k in ('input_tokens', 'cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens', 'output_tokens'))
  107|     w5, w1 = u.get('ephemeral_5m_input_tokens') or 0, u.get('ephemeral_1h_input_tokens') or 0
  108|     rate = (max if d.get('remainder_rate', 'max') == 'max' else min)(per_token(r['cache_write_5m']), per_token(r['cache_write_1h']))
  109|     writes = cw * rate if d.get('split') == 'ignored' else (w5 * per_token(r['cache_write_5m']) + w1 * per_token(r['cache_write_1h']) + (cw - w5 - w1) * rate)
  110|     return i * per_token(r['input']) + cr * per_token(r['cache_read']) + writes + o * per_token(r['output'])
  111| 
  112| 
  113| def hsum(items):
  114|     """Sum of (low, kind, high) exposures with AtLeast propagation (contract 2)."""
  115|     low = sum(x[0] for x in items); high = sum(x[2] for x in items)
  116|     return low, ('at_least' if any(x[1] == 'at_least' for x in items) else 'exact'), high
  117| 
  118| 
  119| def esum_of(e): return (nano(e['low']), e['high']['kind'], nano(e['high']['nano']))
  120| 
  121| 
  122| def att_exp(a):
  123|     x = a['exposure']
  124|     if x['pool'] == 'codex': return None
  125|     return (nano(x['low']['nano']), x['high']['kind'], nano(x['high']['nano']))
  126| 
  127| 
  128| def parse(line):
  129|     try: row = json.loads(line) if isinstance(line, str) else None
  130|     except ValueError: return None
  131|     return row if isinstance(row, dict) else None
  132| 
  133| 
  134| def source_lines(c):
  135|     """{ref: raw line} and {source: [refs in append order]}."""
  136|     lines, order = {}, {}
  137|     for s in c['inputs']['sessions']:
  138|         for turn, log in s['logs'].items():
  139|             src = f'{s["alias"]}/{turn}'
  140|             for n, ln in enumerate(log['lines'], 1):
  141|                 lines[f'{src}:{n}'] = ln; order.setdefault(src, []).append(f'{src}:{n}')
  142|     return lines, order
  143| 
  144| 
  145| def forwarded_row(line):
  146|     """Contract 6.1 (1.2-s1): a provider response exists."""
  147|     row = parse(line)
  148|     if row is None: return False
  149|     if row.get('event') in ('response_model_refused', 'stream_error'): return True
  150|     if row.get('event') != 'request': return False
  151|     st = row.get('http_status')
  152|     if isinstance(st, int) and not isinstance(st, bool): return True
  153|     if row.get('response_id') or row.get('response_model'): return True
  154|     u = row.get('usage')
  155|     return isinstance(u, dict) and any(v is not None for v in u.values())
  156| 
  157| 
  158| def reopened_of(session):
  159|     """Contract L-5: a deliver follows a retire anywhere in the lifecycle rows."""
  160|     seen = False
  161|     for r in session['lifecycle']:
  162|         if r['event'] == 'retire': seen = True
  163|         elif r['event'] == 'deliver' and seen: return True
  164|     return False
  165| 
  166| 
  167| def closure_state(o, ref, profile='current_producer'):
  168|     """Contract 12.4 step 1 (1.3-s1): 'open', 'closed' or 'claimed' (claimed but not established)."""
  169|     ca, ce, cr = o.get('closed_at'), o.get('close'), o.get('closure_record')
  170|     if ca is None and ce is None and cr is None: return 'open'
  171|     rows = [r for a in o.get('admissions', []) for r in a['rows']]
  172|     coherent = isinstance(ca, str) and STAMP.match(ca) is not None and ca >= o['started_at'] and ca <= ref and \
  173|         not any(isinstance(r.get('at'), str) and r['at'] > ca for r in rows)
  174|     if profile == 'current_producer':      # legacy cooperative trust: a consistent scope_budget close entry
  175|         ok = coherent and isinstance(ce, dict) and ce.get('at') == ca and cr is None
  176|     else:                                  # future vertical: the objective's declared admission authority
  177|         auth = o.get('admission_authority') or {}
  178|         ok = coherent and isinstance(cr, dict) and auth.get('kind') == VERTICAL and cr.get('authority_id') == auth.get('id') and \
  179|             cr.get('at') == ca and isinstance(cr.get('receipt'), dict) and cr['receipt'].get('ref') and re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(cr['receipt'].get('sha256')))
  180|     return 'closed' if ok else 'claimed'
  181| 
  182| 
  183| def gate_qualification(c, o, adm, purpose, ref, lifecycle_by_sid, sessions_by_sid, cap_nano=None):
  184|     """Contract 2.3 G-1..G-7 for one admission's session. purpose: 'release' (retired) or 'bound' (open/pending).
  185|     Returns (qualified, reasons, contradicted); a qualified 'bound' gate also sets gate_qualification.ceiling."""
  186|     gate_qualification.ceiling = None
  187|     gid = adm.get('gate_id')
  188|     if not gid: return False, ['NoGateEvidence'], False
  189|     g = next((x for x in c['inputs'].get('gates', []) if x.get('gate_id') == gid), None)
  190|     if not isinstance(g, dict) or not all(k in g for k in ('authority', 'session', 'scope', 'effective_from', 'effective_until', 'writer', 'mechanisms')):
  191|         return False, ['GateMalformed'], False
  192|     reasons = []; auth = o.get('admission_authority') or {}
  193|     witness = c.get('trusted_fixture_inputs', {}).get('gate_verifications', {}).get(gid)
  194|     payload = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(g, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
  195|     if not isinstance(witness, dict): reasons.append('GateVerificationMissing')
  196|     elif witness.get('source') != 'test_mock' or not witness.get('policy_ref') or not witness.get('verification_evidence'):
  197|         reasons.append('GateVerificationRejected')
  198|     elif witness.get('payload_sha256') != payload or witness.get('authority_id') != (g.get('authority') or {}).get('id') or witness.get('session') != g.get('session'):
  199|         reasons.append('GateVerificationBinding')
  200|     ceiling = None
  201|     if g['writer'] != 'authority': reasons.append('GateWriterNotAuthority')
  202|     if (g['authority'] or {}).get('kind') != VERTICAL or (g['authority'] or {}).get('id') != auth.get('id') or auth.get('kind') != VERTICAL: reasons.append('GateAuthorityMismatch')
  203|     if g['session'] != adm['session']: reasons.append('GateSessionMismatch')
  204|     if (g['scope'] or {}).get('kind') != 'session': reasons.append('GateScopeInsufficient')
  205|     launched = [r.get('at') for r in adm['rows'] if r.get('event') in ('launched', 'objective_admission') and isinstance(r.get('at'), str)]
  206|     if not (isinstance(g['effective_from'], str) and STAMP.match(g['effective_from']) and launched and g['effective_from'] <= min(launched)) or \
  207|             not (g['effective_until'] is None or (isinstance(g['effective_until'], str) and g['effective_until'] >= ref)):
  208|         reasons.append('GatePeriodInsufficient')
  209|     need = ('new_forwarding', 'retries', 'reopening') if purpose == 'release' else ('billing_bound', 'retries')
  210|     m = g['mechanisms'] or {}
  211|     if not all((m.get(k) or {}).get('enforced') is True for k in need): reasons.append('GateMechanismMissing')
  212|     if any((m.get(k) or {}).get('enforced') is True and not ((m[k].get('receipt') or {}).get('ref') and re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str((m[k].get('receipt') or {}).get('sha256'))))
  213|            for k in need): reasons.append('GateReceiptMissing')
  214|     if purpose == 'bound' and (m.get('billing_bound') or {}).get('enforced') is True:   # G-8 (1.4-s1, A-03)
  215|         bnd = m['billing_bound'].get('bound')
  216|         if not isinstance(bnd, dict) or not (isinstance(bnd.get('amount_nano'), str) and re.fullmatch(r'\d+', bnd['amount_nano'])): reasons.append('GateBoundMissing')
  217|         else:
  218|             if cap_nano is None or int(bnd['amount_nano']) > cap_nano: reasons.append('GateBoundExceedsCap')
  219|             if bnd.get('currency') != 'USD': reasons.append('GateBoundCurrency')
  220|             if bnd.get('scope') != 'session' or sorted(bnd.get('covers') or []) != COVERS: reasons.append('GateBoundCoverage')
  221|             doc = {'amount_nano': bnd['amount_nano'], 'authority_id': (g['authority'] or {}).get('id'), 'covers': sorted(bnd.get('covers') or []), 'currency': bnd.get('currency'),
  222|                    'effective_from': g['effective_from'], 'effective_until': g['effective_until'], 'gate_id': gid, 'session': g['session']}
  223|             if bnd.get('binding_sha256') != hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(doc, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode()).hexdigest(): reasons.append('GateBoundBinding')
  224|             ceiling = int(bnd['amount_nano'])
  225|     contradicted = False
  226|     sess = sessions_by_sid.get(adm['session'])
  227|     if sess is not None and (m.get('reopening') or {}).get('enforced') is True:
  228|         seen = False
  229|         for r in sess['lifecycle']:
  230|             if r['event'] == 'retire': seen = True
  231|             elif r['event'] == 'deliver' and seen and isinstance(r.get('at'), str) and isinstance(g['effective_from'], str) and r['at'] >= g['effective_from']:
  232|                 contradicted = True
  233|     if contradicted: reasons.append('GateContradicted')
  234|     if not reasons: gate_qualification.ceiling = ceiling
  235|     return (not reasons), reasons, contradicted
  236| 
  237| 
  238| def gate_projection(c, adm, qualified, reasons):
  239|     out = {'qualified': qualified, 'reasons': reasons}
  240|     if adm.get('gate_id'):
  241|         w = c.get('trusted_fixture_inputs', {}).get('gate_verifications', {}).get(adm['gate_id'])
  242|         out['verification_source'] = 'test_mock' if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get('source') == 'test_mock' else 'unverified'
  243|         if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get('payload_sha256'): out['verification_payload_sha256'] = w['payload_sha256']
  244|     return out
  245| 
  246| 
  247| # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ corpus checks
  248| def check_case(c, rates, F):
  249|     cid = c.get('case_id', '?')
  250|     for k in ('schema', 'contract', 'case_id', 'group', 'role', 'class', 'reachability', 'findings', 'provenance', 'invariants',
  251|               'controls', 'inputs', 'evaluations', 'expected', 'ambiguity', 'notes', 'historical', 'rationale', 'revisions', 'revisions_r3',
  252|               'revisions_r4', 'revisions_r5', 'revisions_r6', 'revisions_r7', 'profile'):
  253|         if k not in c: F.add(cid, f'missing field {k}')
  254|     if c.get('schema') != SCHEMA or c.get('contract') != CONTRACT: F.add(cid, 'schema or contract version mismatch')
  255|     if c.get('class') not in CLASSES: F.add(cid, f'bad class {c.get("class")}')
  256|     if c.get('reachability') not in REACH: F.add(cid, 'bad reachability')
  257|     if c.get('role') not in ROLES: F.add(cid, 'bad role')
  258|     if not c.get('provenance', {}).get('origin'): F.add(cid, 'provenance.origin missing')
  259|     for inv in c.get('invariants', []):
  260|         if inv not in INVARIANTS: F.add(cid, f'unknown invariant {inv}')
  261|     if c.get('role') == 'finding' and not c.get('controls'): F.add(cid, 'finding without a control')
  262|     prof = c.get('profile')
  263|     if prof not in PROFILES: F.add(cid, f'bad profile {prof}')
  264|     if prof == 'current_producer':            # OQ-1: no hypothetical gate or authority evidence on current-producer cases
  265|         if c['inputs'].get('gates') or any(o.get('admission_authority') or o.get('closure_record') or any(a.get('gate_id') for a in o['admissions'])
  266|                                             for e in c['evaluations'] for o in e['objectives']):
  267|             F.add(cid, 'current_producer case carries future gate or authority evidence (OQ-1)')
  268|         if c.get('class') == 'future_vertical': F.add(cid, 'current_producer case classed future_vertical')
  269|     if prof == 'future_qualified_gate':
  270|         if c.get('reachability') != 'producer_unreachable' or c.get('class') != 'future_vertical' or c.get('historical') != {}:
  271|             F.add(cid, 'future_qualified_gate case must be producer_unreachable, class future_vertical, with no historical comparison')
  272|     for r in c.get('revisions', []) + c.get('revisions_r3', []) + c.get('revisions_r4', []) + c.get('revisions_r5', []) + c.get('revisions_r6', []) + c.get('revisions_r7', []):
  273|         if set(r) != {'rule', 'field', 'from', 'to'} or not r['rule']: F.add(cid, f'malformed revision entry {r}')
  274|     if c.get('added_in') not in (None,) + tuple(sorted(ADDED_IN)): F.add(cid, 'bad added_in')
  275|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.2-s1' and (c.get('revisions') or c.get('revisions_r3')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 3 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  276|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.3-s1' and (c.get('revisions') or c.get('revisions_r3') or c.get('revisions_r4')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 4 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  277|     if c.get('added_in') == '1.4-s1' and any(c.get(k) for k in ('revisions', 'revisions_r3', 'revisions_r4', 'revisions_r5')): F.add(cid, 'a case added in revision 5 has no earlier expectation to revise')
  278|     inp = c['inputs']
  279|     for name, (digest, _) in rates.items():
  280|         if inp['rates'].get(name) != digest: F.add(cid, f'rate digest mismatch for {name}')
  281|     sessions = {s['alias']: s for s in inp['sessions']}
  282|     for s in inp['sessions']:
  283|         for turn, log in s['logs'].items():
  284|             if not isinstance(log.get('lines'), list) or not isinstance(log.get('final_newline'), bool): F.add(cid, f'bad log {s["alias"]}/{turn}')
  285|     lines, order = source_lines(c)
  286|     ex = c['expected']
  287|     covered, keys = [], set()
  288|     for a in ex['attempts']:
  289|         check_attempt(cid, c, a, lines, rates, F)
  290|         keys.add(a['key']); covered += a['evidence']
  291|     for x in ex['non_attempt'] + ex['unreadable']:
  292|         covered.append(x['evidence'])
  293|         for b in x.get('blocking', []):
  294|             if b not in BLOCKING: F.add(cid, f'unknown blocking {b}')
  295|     for r in covered:
  296|         if r not in lines: F.add(cid, f'evidence ref {r} not in inputs')
  297|     dup = {r for r in covered if covered.count(r) > 1}
  298|     if dup: F.add(cid, f'evidence counted twice (INV-F6): {sorted(dup)}')
  299|     missing = set(lines) - set(covered)
  300|     if missing: F.add(cid, f'evidence not accounted for (INV-F6): {sorted(missing)}')
  301|     for alias in sessions:
  302|         if alias not in ex['lifecycle']: F.add(cid, f'lifecycle missing for {alias}')
  303|     for alias, l in ex['lifecycle'].items():
  304|         if l.get('state') not in LIFE or l.get('clock') not in ('trusted', 'untrusted') or not isinstance(l.get('reopened'), bool):
  305|             F.add(cid, f'bad lifecycle {alias}'); continue
  306|         s = sessions.get(alias)
  307|         if s is None: continue                           # S08 pair: journal-only cases (revision-1 oddity)
  308|         if l['reopened'] != reopened_of(s): F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: reopened {l["reopened"]} contradicts its rows (L-5, R-10)')
  309|         last = s['lifecycle'][-1] if s['lifecycle'] else None
  310|         if l['state'] == 'retired' and not (last and last['event'] == 'retire' and last.get('at')):
  311|             F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: retired but the last row is not a dated retire (L-4)')
  312|         if l['state'] == 'open' and last and last['event'] == 'retire' and last.get('at') and l['clock'] == 'trusted':
  313|             F.add(cid, f'lifecycle {alias}: open but the last row is a dated retire (L-4)')
  314|     names = [e['name'] for e in c['evaluations']]
  315|     if len(names) != len(set(names)): F.add(cid, 'duplicate evaluation names')
  316|     for e in c['evaluations']:
  317|         check_evaluation(cid, c, e, keys, sessions, lines, order, F)
  318| 
  319| 
  320| def check_attempt(cid, c, a, lines, rates, F):
  321|     k = a['key']; m = KEY.match(k)
  322|     if not m: F.add(cid, f'bad key {k}'); return
  323|     kind, alias, turn, field, num = m.groups()
  324|     src = f'{alias}/turn-{turn}'
  325|     rows = [parse(lines.get(r)) for r in a['evidence']]
  326|     if kind == 'row' and f'{src}:{num}' not in a['evidence']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: row key line not in its evidence')
  327|     if kind in ('res', 'adm') and not any(r and r.get('event') in ('usd_reserved', 'subscription_proxy_admitted') and str(r.get('rid')) == num for r in rows):
  328|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: no reservation/admission with that rid in its evidence')
  329|     corr = a['correlation']                                                       # R-06: key join plus auxiliary rows
  330|     if corr.get('kind') not in KEY_JOIN: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad key join {corr.get("kind")}')
  331|     for ax in corr.get('auxiliary', []):
  332|         row = parse(lines.get(ax.get('evidence')))
  333|         if ax.get('evidence') not in a['evidence'] or ax.get('rule') not in SHAPE_RULES or row is None: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad auxiliary {ax}'); continue
  334|         want = {'RS-1': row.get('event') == 'stream_error' and 'detail' in row, 'RS-2': row.get('event') == 'stream_error' and 'detail' not in row,
  335|                 'RS-3': row.get('event') == 'response_model_refused'}[ax['rule']]
  336|         if not want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: auxiliary {ax["rule"]} does not match its row shape (E-3)')
  337|     s = a['settlement']
  338|     if s.get('decision') not in DECISIONS: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad decision'); return
  339|     if s['decision'] == 'valid' and s.get('basis') not in BASES: F.add(cid, f'{k}: valid without a basis')
  340|     if s['decision'] != 'rejected' and s.get('reasons'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: reasons on a non-rejected decision')
  341|     if s['decision'] == 'rejected' and not s.get('reasons'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: rejected without reasons')
  342|     for r in s.get('reasons', []):
  343|         if r not in REJECT: F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown reject reason {r}')
  344|     ob = a['obligation']; obt = (ob.get('state'), ob.get('amount_source'))
  345|     if obt not in OBLIGATION: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad obligation {obt}')
  346|     rp_want = replacement_of(a)
  347|     if a.get('replacement') != rp_want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: replacement {a.get("replacement")} != {rp_want} (2.1, A-01, INV-F13)')
  348|     want = {'valid': {'usage': ('closed', 'settlement_amount' if rp_want['eligible'] else 'reservation_bound'), 'reservation': ('closed', 'reservation_confirmed'),
  349|                       'upstream_error': ('closed', 'reservation_confirmed'), 'error_status': ('closed', 'reservation_bound')}.get(s.get('basis')),
  350|             'absent': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'), 'rejected': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'),
  351|             'conflicting': ('outstanding', 'reservation_bound'), 'not_applicable': ('none', 'none')}[s['decision']]
  352|     if kind == 'row' and s['decision'] in ('rejected', 'conflicting'): want = ('none', 'none')
  353|     if ob.get('state') == 'closed' and ob.get('by') not in [ref for ref, row in zip(a['evidence'], rows) if row and isinstance(row.get('usd'), dict)]: F.add(cid, f'{k}: closed obligation lacks settlement evidence by reference')
  354|     if obt != want: F.add(cid, f'{k}: obligation {obt} does not match decision (want {want}) (INV-F1)')
  355|     if ob.get('state') == 'closed' and not (s['decision'] == 'valid' and corr.get('kind') == 'exact'):
  356|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: closed without a Valid settlement on an Exact key join (INV-F1, R-06)')
  357|     for b in a['blocking']:
  358|         if b not in BLOCKING and not re.fullmatch(r'MalformedEvidence\([A-Za-z_]+\)', b): F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown blocking {b}')
  359|     for d in a['diagnostics']:
  360|         if d not in DIAGNOSTICS: F.add(cid, f'{k}: unknown diagnostic {d}')
  361|     if s['decision'] == 'rejected' and 'SettlementRejected' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: rejected without SettlementRejected')
  362|     if s['decision'] == 'conflicting' and 'SettlementConflict' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: conflicting without SettlementConflict')
  363|     fw = a.get('forwarding')                                                       # R-13
  364|     if fw not in FORWARDING: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad or missing forwarding fact')
  365|     elif fw != ('forwarded' if any(forwarded_row(lines.get(r)) for r in a['evidence']) else 'unknown'):
  366|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: forwarding {fw} contradicts its evidence rows (6.1, R-13)')
  367|     ex_ = a.get('execution')                                                       # R-07
  368|     if ex_ not in EXECUTION: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad or missing execution fact')
  369|     elif ex_ != ('terminal' if any(r and r.get('event') in TERMINAL_EVENTS for r in rows) else 'not_terminal'):
  370|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: execution {ex_} contradicts its evidence rows (6.2)')
  371|     raw_settlement_checks(cid, c, a, rows, lines, rates, F)
  372|     v = a['valuation']; st = v.get('state')
  373|     if st == 'valued':
  374|         lows, highs = [], []
  375|         for d in v['derivations']:
  376|             try: p = price(d, rates)
  377|             except (KeyError, ValueError) as e: F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation not computable: {e}'); continue
  378|             if p != nano(d['nano']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation {d["model"]} states {d["nano"]}, rate files give {p}')
  379|             if d['provider'] + '/' + d['model'] not in v['identities']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: derivation model {d["model"]} not among identities')
  380|             if d['for'] in ('low', 'both'): lows.append(p)
  381|             if d['for'] in ('high', 'both'): highs.append(p)
  382|             if d['provider'] == 'anthropic' and d.get('split') != 'ignored':
  383|                 u = d['usage']
  384|                 rem = (u.get('cache_creation_input_tokens') or 0) - (u.get('ephemeral_5m_input_tokens') or 0) - (u.get('ephemeral_1h_input_tokens') or 0)
  385|                 if rem > 0 and d['for'] in ('low', 'high') and d.get('remainder_rate') != {'low': 'min', 'high': 'max'}[d['for']]:
  386|                     F.add(cid, f'{k}: residual Anthropic writes must be at min for the low and max for the high (V-4, R-15)')
  387|         if lows and min(lows) != nano(v['low']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation low {v["low"]} != derivations {min(lows)}')
  388|         if highs and max(highs) != nano(v['high']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation high {v["high"]} != derivations {max(highs)}')
  389|         if nano(v['low']) > nano(v['high']): F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation low > high')
  390|         if len(v['identities']) > 1 and 'IdentityContradiction' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: several identities without IdentityContradiction (D6)')
  391|     tiers = set(rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['tier_multiplier'])             # R-03, from raw rows
  392|     oa_req = [r for r in rows if r and r.get('event') == 'request' and r.get('provider', 'openai') != 'anthropic' and isinstance(r.get('usage'), dict)
  393|               and any(x is not None for x in r['usage'].values())]
  394|     if st == 'valued' and any(d['provider'] == 'openai' for d in v['derivations']) and any(
  395|             not (isinstance(r.get('observed_tier'), str) and r['observed_tier'] in tiers) for r in oa_req):
  396|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: OpenAI tier missing or unrecognised but valued as a finite range (V-3, R-03)')
  397|     x = a['exposure']
  398|     bl = a.get('billing')
  399|     if x['pool'] == 'codex':
  400|         if set(x) != {'pool', 'billing'}: F.add(cid, f'{k}: codex pool carries money (INV-F9)')
  401|         if bl != 'not_applicable': F.add(cid, f'{k}: codex attempt billing must be not_applicable')
  402|         return
  403|     lo, kd, hi = att_exp(a)
  404|     if x['low']['basis'] not in LOW_BASES or x['high']['basis'] not in HIGH_BASES: F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad basis')
  405|     if kd not in ('exact', 'at_least'): F.add(cid, f'{k}: bad high kind')
  406|     if lo > hi: F.add(cid, f'{k}: low > high (INV-F11)')
  407|     if kd == 'at_least' and 'UnresolvedExposure' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: AtLeast without UnresolvedExposure (D5)')
  408|     if lo > 0 and x['low']['basis'] != 'usage_valuation': F.add(cid, f'{k}: non-zero low not supported by usage valuation (INV-F11)')
  409|     if bl not in BILLING or bl != ('established' if kd == 'exact' else 'unresolved'):
  410|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: billing {bl} does not follow the high kind {kd} (INV-F12, R-07)')
  411|     table_x(cid, k, a, lo, kd, hi, F, lines)
  412| 
  413| 
  414| def usage_bound(a):
  415|     """FB-1: a complete usage valuation exists (as the expected valuation states it)."""
  416|     s, v = a['settlement'], a['valuation']
  417|     if v.get('state') != 'valued' or not v.get('complete'): return None
  418|     if s['decision'] == 'valid' and s.get('basis') == 'usage': return nano(v['high'])
  419|     if s['decision'] == 'not_applicable': return nano(v['high'])
  420|     return None
  421| 
  422| 
  423| def replacement_of(a):
  424|     """2.1 (1.4-s1): Eligible iff Valid(Usage) with a complete usage valuation over established sets."""
  425|     s_, v, x = a['settlement'], a['valuation'], a['exposure']
  426|     if x.get('pool') == 'codex' or a['obligation'].get('state') == 'none': return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'NoReservation'}
  427|     if not (s_.get('decision') == 'valid' and s_.get('basis') == 'usage'): return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'NotUsageSettlement'}
  428|     if v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): return {'eligible': True, 'reason': 'UsageBound'}
  429|     return {'eligible': False, 'reason': 'UsageIncomplete' if v.get('state') == 'valued' else 'UsageUnpriceable'}
  430| 
  431| 
  432| def high_of(terms):
  433|     terms = [t for t in terms if t[0] is not None]
  434|     best = max(t[0] for t in terms)
  435|     return best, sorted((t for t in terms if t[0] == best), key=lambda t: TIE[t[1]])[0][1]
  436| 
  437| 
  438| def raw_reservation(a, lines):
  439|     """X-0 (1.4-s1, A-09): the largest distinct well-formed reservation amount in the attempt's own rows."""
  440|     vals = set(); n = 0; seen = set()
  441|     for r in a['evidence']:
  442|         row = parse(lines.get(r))
  443|         if row and row.get('event') == 'usd_reserved':
  444|             key = json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True)
  445|             if key in seen: continue
  446|             seen.add(key); n += 1
  447|             amt = row.get('reserved_usd')
  448|             if isinstance(amt, (int, float)) and not isinstance(amt, bool): vals.add(int(Decimal(str(amt)) * 10 ** 9))
  449|     return (max(vals) if vals else None), n
  450| 
  451| 
  452| def table_x(cid, k, a, lo, kd, hi, F, lines=None):
  453|     """Consistency of expected exposure with TRANSITIONS Table X (1.4-s1), including the X-0 basis on ties."""
  454|     s, v = a['settlement'], a['valuation']
  455|     R = nano(s['reserved']) if 'reserved' in s else None    # settlement.reserved is the settlement's claim; R is the reservation rows' amount
  456|     if lines is not None:
  457|         Rraw, nres = raw_reservation(a, lines)
  458|         if Rraw is not None: R = Rraw                                               # X-0 (1.4-s1): the largest distinct reservation (A-09)
  459|     ch = nano(s['charged']) if 'charged' in s else None
  460|     vl, vh = (nano(v['low']), nano(v['high'])) if v.get('state') == 'valued' else (None, None)
  461|     ph = nano(v['priced_high']) if v.get('state') == 'unpriceable' and v.get('priced_high') is not None else None
  462|     sl = nano(v['supported_low']) if v.get('state') == 'unpriceable' and v.get('supported_low') is not None else 0
  463|     known_v = vh if vh is not None else ph
  464|     exp = None; terms = None
  465|     d, b = s['decision'], s.get('basis')
  466|     if d == 'valid' and b == 'usage' and ch is not None:
  467|         if v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): terms = [(ch, 'proxy_charge'), (vh, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl, 'exact')     # FB-1, eligible
  468|         elif R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (ch, 'proxy_charge'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')  # A-01
  469|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'reservation' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')
  470|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'upstream_error' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')
  471|     elif d == 'valid' and b == 'error_status' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl if vl is not None else sl, 'at_least')   # A-02
  472|     elif d == 'absent' and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')
  473|     elif d in ('rejected', 'conflicting') and R is not None: terms = [(R, 'proxy_reservation'), (ch, 'proxy_charge'), (known_v, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (0, 'at_least')  # R-05
  474|     elif d == 'not_applicable' and v.get('state') == 'valued' and v.get('complete'): terms = [(vh, 'usage_valuation')]; exp = (vl, 'exact')
  475|     if exp is not None:
  476|         h_amt, h_basis = high_of(terms)
  477|         if (exp[0], exp[1], h_amt) != (lo, kd, hi): F.add(cid, f'{k}: exposure {(lo, kd, hi)} != Table X {(exp[0], exp[1], h_amt)}')
  478|         elif a['exposure']['high'].get('basis') != h_basis: F.add(cid, f'{k}: high basis {a["exposure"]["high"].get("basis")} != X-0 tie order {h_basis}')
  479|     ub = usage_bound(a)
  480|     if kd == 'exact' and ub is None: F.add(cid, f'{k}: Exact high without a usage bound (FB-1, FB-2)')
  481|     if kd == 'exact' and a['exposure']['high']['basis'] == 'proxy_charge' and not (ub is not None and ub <= hi):
  482|         F.add(cid, f'{k}: Exact high with basis proxy_charge needs a usage bound below it (FB-1, R-02)')
  483| 
  484| 
  485| def openai_range(u, model, mult, rates):
  486|     """V-4 (1.4-s1, A-04) from raw counts: (low, high) over the vertices of unknown cache counts, or 'malformed'."""
  487|     oa = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]; r = oa['models'][model]
  488|     i, o = u.get('input_tokens'), u.get('output_tokens')
  489|     if not all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and x >= 0 for x in (i, o)): return 'malformed'
  490|     def state(key):
  491|         if key not in u or u[key] is None: return None
  492|         x = u[key]
  493|         return x if isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) and x >= 0 else 'malformed'
  494|     c, w = state('cached_tokens'), state('cache_write_tokens')
  495|     if 'malformed' in (c, w): return 'malformed'
  496|     if c is not None and w is not None and c + w > i: return 'malformed'
  497|     if c is not None and w is not None: pts = [(c, w)]
  498|     elif c is not None: pts = [(c, 0), (c, i - c)]
  499|     elif w is not None: pts = [(0, w), (i - w, w)]
  500|     else: pts = [(0, 0), (i, 0), (0, i)]
  501|     long_ = i > int(oa['long_input_threshold'])
  502|     mi = Fraction(oa['long_input_multiplier']) if long_ else 1; mo = Fraction(oa['long_output_multiplier']) if long_ else 1
  503|     m = Fraction(Decimal(mult))
  504|     prices = [(i - cc - ww) * per_token(r['input'], m * mi) + cc * per_token(r['cached_input'], m * mi) + ww * per_token(r['cache_write'], m * mi)
  505|               + o * per_token(r['output'], m * mo) for cc, ww in pts]
  506|     return min(prices), max(prices)
  507| 
  508| 
  509| def raw_usage_policy_checks(cid, a, rows, F):
  510|     """Root P1/P2 assertions; auxiliary counts do not establish a billing report."""
  511|     requests = [r for r in rows if r and r.get('event') == 'request']
  512|     v = a['valuation']; k = a['key']
  513|     for row in requests:
  514|         fam = PROVIDERS.get(row.get('provider', 'openai'))
  515|         billing = ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cached_tokens', 'cache_write_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens')
  516|         u = row.get('usage')
  517|         if not isinstance(u, dict):
  518|             if u is not None and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: non-object usage valued (UsageShape)')
  519|             continue
  520|         report = any(u.get(f) is not None for f in billing)
  521|         if a['settlement']['decision'] not in ('rejected', 'conflicting'):
  522|             for f in billing:
  523|                 if u.get(f) is not None and not (type(u[f]) is int and u[f] >= 0) and 'MalformedEvidence(' + f + ')' not in a['blocking']:
  524|                     F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed billing evidence lacks field diagnostic (P2)')
  525| 
  526|         contrary = ('cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('cached_tokens', 'cache_write_tokens')
  527|         mismatch = fam is None or any(u.get(f) is not None for f in contrary)
  528|         if mismatch and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: incompatible provider billing schema priced (UsageSchemaMismatch)')
  529|         if not report and len(requests) == 1 and v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: auxiliary-only usage cannot establish priceable report (P2)')
  530|         if fam == 'anthropic' and v.get('state') == 'valued':
  531|             tier = row.get('observed_tier')
  532|             if tier is not None and tier != 'standard': F.add(cid, f'{k}: unestablished Anthropic tier priced (P2)')
  533|         if fam == 'anthropic' and v.get('complete'):
  534|             total = u.get('cache_creation_input_tokens')
  535|             parts = [u.get(f) if u.get(f) is not None else 0 for f in ('ephemeral_5m_input_tokens', 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens')]
  536|             if type(total) is not int or any(type(x) is not int or x < 0 for x in parts) or sum(parts) > total:
  537|                 F.add(cid, f'{k}: inconsistent lifetime split cannot be complete (P2)')
  538|         if v.get('complete'):
  539|             status = row.get('http_status'); success = row.get('terminal') in ({'response.completed', 'response.done'} if fam == 'openai' else {'message_stop'})
  540|             required = ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens') if fam == 'openai' else ('input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'cache_read_input_tokens', 'cache_creation_input_tokens')
  541|             counts = all(isinstance(u.get(f), int) and not isinstance(u.get(f), bool) and u[f] >= 0 for f in required)
  542|             if not (type(status) is int and status == 200 and success and counts and not mismatch): F.add(cid, f'{k}: complete valuation lacks status+terminal+counts conjunction (P2)')
  543|         for f in ('reasoning_tokens',) if fam == 'openai' else ():
  544|             if u.get(f) is not None and not (type(u[f]) is int and u[f] >= 0):
  545|                 if 'MalformedEvidence(' + f + ')' not in a['blocking']: F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed auxiliary evidence lacks field diagnostic (P2)')
  546| 
  547| 
  548| def raw_settlement_checks(cid, c, a, rows, lines, rates, F):
  549|     """Settlement conditions and valuation re-derived from raw producer rows where the contract makes them mechanical:
  550|     status by path (5.7, A-06), model canonicalisation (MC-1..MC-3, A-05), and the OpenAI valuation range (V-4, A-04)."""
  551|     raw_usage_policy_checks(cid, a, rows, F)
  552|     k = a['key']; s_ = a['settlement']; d, b = s_.get('decision'), s_.get('basis')
  553|     setl = [r for r in rows if r and isinstance(r.get('usd'), dict) and r.get('event') in ('request', 'upstream_error')]
  554|     distinct_reservations = {lines[ref] for ref in a['evidence'] if isinstance(parse(lines.get(ref)), dict) and parse(lines[ref]).get('event') == 'usd_reserved'}
  555|     if len(distinct_reservations) != 1 and setl:
  556|         forbidden = set(s_.get('reasons', [])) & {'ReservedMismatch', 'Order', 'KeepAll'}
  557|         if forbidden: F.add(cid, f'{k}: reservation-relative checks inapplicable without UniqueReservation: {sorted(forbidden)} (P3)')
  558|         if d == 'valid': F.add(cid, f'{k}: valid settlement without UniqueReservation (P3)')
  559| 
  560|     if d == 'valid' and setl:
  561|         row = setl[0]; st = row.get('http_status', '__absent__')
  562|         isint = isinstance(st, int) and not isinstance(st, bool)
  563|         ok = {'usage': isint and st == 200, 'error_status': isint and st != 200, 'reservation': (isint if row.get('event') == 'request' else st == '__absent__'),
  564|               'upstream_error': row.get('event') == 'upstream_error' and st == '__absent__'}.get(b, True)
  565|         if not ok: F.add(cid, f'{k}: Valid({b}) but the settlement row status {st!r} is not accepted on this path (5.7, A-06)')
  566|         if b == 'usage':
  567|             alias = k.split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  568|             launch = next((s2.get('launch') for s2 in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s2['alias'] == alias), None) or {}
  569|             lm = launch.get('model') if isinstance(launch.get('model'), str) else ''
  570|             prov, _, mid = lm.partition('/')
  571|             if prov not in PROVIDERS or not mid or row.get('model') != mid or PROVIDERS.get(row.get('provider', 'openai')) != PROVIDERS.get(prov):
  572|                 F.add(cid, f'{k}: Valid(Usage) but the request model {row.get("model")!r} does not equal the canonical pinned id of {lm!r} (MC-1..MC-3, A-05)')
  573|     # OpenAI valuation range from raw counts (single priced identity, recognised tier)
  574|     tiers = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['tier_multiplier']; models = rates['openai_api_rates.json'][1]['models']
  575|     v = a['valuation']
  576|     for row in rows:
  577|         if not (row and row.get('event') == 'request' and row.get('provider', 'openai') != 'anthropic' and isinstance(row.get('usage'), dict)): continue
  578|         u = row['usage']
  579|         if not any(x is not None for x in u.values()): continue
  580|         tier = row.get('observed_tier')
  581|         ids = {x for x in (row.get('model'), row.get('response_model')) if x}
  582|         if len(ids) != 1 or not (isinstance(tier, str) and tier in tiers): continue
  583|         model = next(iter(ids))
  584|         if model not in models: continue
  585|         rng = openai_range(u, model, str(tiers[tier]), rates)
  586|         if rng != 'malformed' and v.get('state') == 'not_reported':
  587|             F.add(cid, f'{k}: priceable usage in its rows but valuation not_reported (V is independent of the decision, A-06/S06)')
  588|         if rng == 'malformed':
  589|             if v.get('state') == 'valued': F.add(cid, f'{k}: malformed cache counts but valued (V-4, A-04)')
  590|         elif v.get('state') == 'valued' and len(v.get('identities', [])) == 1 and len([x for x in rows if x and x.get('event') == 'request']) == 1:
  591|             if (nano(v['low']), nano(v['high'])) != rng: F.add(cid, f'{k}: valuation {(v["low"], v["high"])} != raw V-4 range {rng} (A-04)')
  592| 
  593| 
  594| def obligation_interval(c, a, lines, order, lifecycle):
  595|     """Contract 11.2 interval with P-2 neighbour bounds: (start_lo, end_hi) as stamps, None meaning unbounded; or
  596|     'unplaceable'. Closed: [reservation, settlement]; Outstanding: [reservation, following dated retirement or open];
  597|     None (unbudgeted, orphan): [first row, last row]."""
  598|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  599|     if lifecycle.get(alias, {}).get('clock') == 'untrusted': return 'unplaceable'
  600|     refs = sorted(a['evidence'], key=lambda r: (r.rsplit(':', 1)[0], int(r.rsplit(':', 1)[1])))
  601|     def dated(ref):
  602|         row = parse(lines.get(ref)); at = row.get('at') if row else None
  603|         return at if isinstance(at, str) and STAMP.match(at) else None
  604|     def bounds(ref):
  605|         at = dated(ref)
  606|         if at: return at, at
  607|         src = ref.rsplit(':', 1)[0]; seq = order[src]; i = seq.index(ref)
  608|         before = next((dated(r) for r in reversed(seq[:i]) if dated(r)), None)
  609|         after = next((dated(r) for r in seq[i + 1:] if dated(r)), None)
  610|         if before and after and before > after: return 'inverted', 'inverted'
  611|         return before, after
  612|     first_lo, _ = bounds(refs[0])
  613|     _, last_hi = bounds(refs[-1])
  614|     if 'inverted' in (first_lo, last_hi): return 'unplaceable'
  615|     if a['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding':
  616|         sess = next(s for s in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s['alias'] == alias)
  617|         rets = [r['at'] for r in sess['lifecycle'] if r['event'] == 'retire' and isinstance(r.get('at'), str) and STAMP.match(r['at'])
  618|                 and (first_lo is None or r['at'] >= first_lo)]
  619|         return first_lo, (min(rets) if rets else None)          # open when no following dated retirement
  620|     return first_lo, last_hi
  621| 
  622| 
  623| def o7_candidates(c, e, a, lines, order, lifecycle, ref):
  624|     """O-7 (1.2-s1): tree members whose window could contain the obligation interval; unplaceable keeps them all."""
  625|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  626|     sid = next(s_['session_id'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['alias'] == alias)
  627|     iv = obligation_interval(c, a, lines, order, lifecycle); out = []
  628|     for o in e['objectives']:
  629|         if sid not in o['tree_sessions']: continue
  630|         if iv != 'unplaceable':
  631|             lo, hi = iv
  632|             closed = o['closed_at'] if closure_state(o, ref, c.get('profile')) == 'closed' else None
  633|             if (hi is not None and hi < o['started_at']) or (closed and lo is not None and lo > closed): continue
  634|         out.append(o['objective'])
  635|     return sorted(out)
  636| 
  637| 
  638| def admitting_objectives(c, e, a):
  639|     alias = a['key'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0]
  640|     sid = next(s_['session_id'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['alias'] == alias)
  641|     return sorted({o['objective'] for o in e['objectives'] for ad in o['admissions'] if ad['session'] == sid})
  642| 
  643| 
  644| def check_evaluation(cid, c, e, keys, sessions, lines, order, F):
  645|     ex, ee = c['expected'], e['expected']
  646|     ref = c['inputs']['reference_time']
  647|     objs = {o['objective']: o for o in e['objectives']}
  648|     attempts = {a['key']: a for a in ex['attempts']}
  649|     for at in ee['attribution']:
  650|         if at['attempt'] not in keys: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: attribution names unknown attempt {at["attempt"]}'); continue
  651|         if at['ownership'] not in OWNERSHIP or at['placement'] not in PLACEMENTS: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: bad attribution {at}')
  652|         if at['ownership'] == 'owned' and at['objective'] not in objs: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: owner {at["objective"]} not an objective')
  653|         a = attempts[at['attempt']]
  654|         if at['ownership'] == 'uncertain':
  655|             if not at.get('candidates'): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: uncertain without candidates (INV-T2)')
  656|             miss = set(admitting_objectives(c, e, a)) - set(at.get('candidates', []))
  657|             if miss: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: uncertain attempt drops admitting objectives {sorted(miss)} (O-3a, R-08)')
  658|         if at['ownership'] == 'unadmitted':
  659|             if at.get('objective') is not None: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted attempt {at["attempt"]} names an owner (O-6)')
  660|             if admitting_objectives(c, e, a): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted although an admission names its session (O-6)')
  661|             want = o7_candidates(c, e, a, lines, order, ex['lifecycle'], ref)
  662|             if sorted(at.get('candidates', [])) != want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: unadmitted candidates {at.get("candidates")} != O-7 {want} (R-12)')
  663|     attributed = [a['attempt'] for a in ee['attribution']]
  664|     for k in keys:
  665|         if attributed.count(k) != 1: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: attempt {k} attributed {attributed.count(k)} times')
  666|     exp_objs = {o['objective']: o for o in ee['objectives']}
  667|     for name in objs:
  668|         if name not in exp_objs: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: no expected result for objective {name}')
  669|     spent = {}
  670|     for at in ee['attribution']:
  671|         if at['ownership'] == 'owned':
  672|             x = att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']])
  673|             if x: spent.setdefault(at['objective'], {}).setdefault(at['attempt'].split(':')[1].split('/')[0], []).append(x)
  674|     for name, oe in exp_objs.items():
  675|         o = objs.get(name)
  676|         if o is None: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}: expected result for unknown objective {name}'); continue
  677|         if oe['status'] not in ('established', 'not_established'): F.add(cid, 'bad status')
  678|         for r in oe['reasons']:
  679|             if r not in OBJ_REASONS and not re.fullmatch(r'MalformedEvidence\([A-Za-z_]+\)', r): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: unknown reason {r}')
  680|         if (oe['status'] == 'established') != (not oe['reasons']): F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: status/reasons disagree')
  681|         owned = [at for at in ee['attribution'] if at['ownership'] == 'owned' and at['objective'] == name]
  682|         want_provisional = financial_provisional(attempts, ee['attribution'], oe)
  683|         if type(oe.get('provisional')) is not bool or oe['provisional'] != want_provisional: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: provisional violates financial unresolved-billing predicate')
  684|         closed = [att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) for at in owned if attempts[at['attempt']]['obligation']['state'] in ('closed', 'none') and att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']])]
  685|         outst = [att_exp(attempts[at['attempt']]) for at in owned if attempts[at['attempt']]['obligation']['state'] == 'outstanding']
  686|         for label, items in (('owned_closed', closed), ('owned_outstanding', outst)):
  687|             want = hsum(items) if items else (0, 'exact', 0)
  688|             if esum_of(oe[label]) != want: F.add(cid, f'{e["name"]}/{name}: {label} {esum_of(oe[label])} != sum of owned attempts {want}')
  689|         implied = set()
  690|         for at in owned:
  691|             implied |= set(attempts[at['attempt']]['blocking'])
  692|             if at['placement'] == 'unplaceable': implied.add('PlacementUnplaceable')
  693|             if at['placement'] in ('straddles_start', 'straddles_closure'): implied.add('PlacementStraddles')
  694|             if at['placement'] in ('before', 'after'): implied.add('AttributionContradiction')
  695|         for at in ee['attribution']:
  696|             if at['ownership'] in ('uncertain', 'unadmitted') and name in at.get('candidates', []) and attempts[at['attempt']]['exposure']['pool'] == 'pi_api':
  697|                 implied.add('OwnershipUncertain' if at['ownership'] == 'uncertain' else 'HistoricalUnadmitted'); implied |= set(attempts[at['attempt']]['blocking'])
  698|         scope = set(o['tree_sessions']) | {a['session'] for a in o['admissions']}
  699|         scope_alias = {s_['alias'] for s_ in c['inputs']['sessions'] if s_['session_id'] in scope}
  700|         for x in ex['non_attempt'] + ex['unreadable']:
  701|             if x['evidence'].split('/')[0] in scope_alias: implied |= set(x.get('blocking', []))
  702|         for x in ex['inventory']:
  703|             if x['session'] in scope_alias: implied |= set(x.get('blocking', []))
